This is a planning document only. It changes no code, schema, Figma, Decision Log or legal text. Every row traces to an audit frame number, a Decision Log number or a code path the audit (or this pass) checked. Where a fact could not be determined it is in Section 6, not guessed.

## 1. Summary

Recounted from the audit frame table (`docs/mvp-coverage-audit.md` section 2, 443 rows parsed):

| Status | Frames |
|---|---:|
| Built | 196 |
| Partial | 71 |
| Stub | 34 |
| Missing | 35 |
| NotInspected | 6 |
| **Screen frames mapped (Built + Partial + Stub + Missing + NotInspected)** | **342** |
| Reference / component / annotation (not screens) | 65 |
| Archived / hidden (not screens) | 36 |
| **All frames in the audit table** | **443** |

Per Decision Log #378, **every Missing, Stub and Partial frame is launch-blocking and in scope** (35 Missing, 34 Stub, 71 Partial). #378 does not mention the 6 NotInspected frames; this register treats them as needing verification before launch (see Section 6).

**Reconciliation.** Gap frames counted in register rows: 146 (= 71 Partial + 34 Stub + 35 Missing + 6 NotInspected). 146 gap + 196 Built = 342 screen frames, which is the audit's "342". Adding Reference (65) and Archived (36) gives 443, the audit's 443 total. Every gap frame appears in exactly one row; no frame is unaccounted for. (The brief's check "rows + Built + Reference + Archived = 342" does not hold literally because 342 already excludes Reference and Archived; see Section 6.)

Register size: **227 B-IDs** (41 carry gap frames, 186 are work items or Figma re-syncs with no gap frame of their own; was 221 = 41 + 180 before Decision Log #414 (B-222 to B-227, Section 40; they carry 0 gap frames), 217 = 41 + 176 before Decision Log #413 (B-218 to B-221, Section 38; they carry 0 gap frames), 208 = 41 + 167 before Decision Log #412 (B-209 to B-217, Section 35; they carry 0 gap frames), 192 = 41 + 151 before Decision Log #411 (B-193 to B-208, Section 32; they carry 0 gap frames), 180 = 41 + 139 before Decision Log #410 (B-181 to B-192, Section 30; they carry 0 gap frames), 178 = 41 + 137 before Decision Log #409 (B-179 and B-180, Section 29; they carry 0 gap frames), 177 = 41 + 136 before item (8) of Decision Log #408 (B-178, Section 28; it carries 0 gap frames), 162 = 41 + 121 before Decision Log #408 (B-163 to B-177, Section 27; they carry 0 gap frames, so the 146-frame reconciliation is unchanged), 139 = 41 + 98 before Decision Log #407 (B-140 to B-162, Section 25), and 126 = 41 + 85 before Decision Log #406 (B-127 to B-139, Section 22; this paragraph and the Waves table were not updated for #406 and still showed 126 until this update, although Section 22d and the Section 3 lists already counted 139); 123 = 41 + 82 before Decision Log #405 (B-124 to B-126, Section 19), 118 = 41 + 77 before Decision Log #404, and 112 = 41 + 71 before Decision Log #403). B-091 to B-106 were added on 2026-10-14 (Section 8) and B-107 to B-112 on 2026-10-16 (Section 10); they carry 0 gap frames, so the 146-frame reconciliation above is unchanged. Decision Log #402 (2026-10-16) added no B-ID and removed none: B-110 was re-scoped in place (Section 11). Decision Log #403 (2026-10-16) added B-113 to B-118; they carry 0 gap frames, so the 146-frame reconciliation is unchanged (Section 12). Built frames that decisions #367-#380 make stale are referenced by Figma re-sync rows but stay counted in the Built bucket.

New hourly GuardianStaleDeclineSweepService: a row declined at decline 1 or 2 (explicit source, fewer than three recorded, active minor) that is not reopened within GUARDIAN_DECLINE_LAPSE_DAYS (default 14) of the new Guardian.consentDeclinedAt is reopened as pending with a fresh token and the one automatic chase spent (consentAutoResentAt), the minor is emailed a warning (new template, [COPY PENDING LEGAL REVIEW]), and when that request lapses the existing expiry sweep closes the account (pending_deletion, 30-day grace). MECHANICS CONFIRMED IN THE CODE: the 14 days start at consentDeclinedAt (a new column: no decline time existed anywhere, so the question could not be answered from the schema); the automatic re-send comes FIRST (the row enters the lapse path at its first stage, not at the second-lapse outcome, because the unanswered path never closes an account without warning the minor and a declined row has had no email at all); an expiry never counts among the three declines (declineCount is untouched; only reason declined increments it). POST /admin/users/:id/dob-correction now rejects a reason containing a date-shaped string with 400 reason_contains_date, before any database work: numeric dates with separators, eight-digit dates, and month names beside a day or a year. | B-140 | Browser sign-up fix (QA D-01, BLOCKER / SAFEGUARDING): restrict CORS to the app origins and stop sending cookie credentials on register - apps/web/src/api/auth.ts registerUser drops credentials: 'include'; services/api/src/main.ts enableCors takes the allowed origins WEB_APP_BASE_URL and a new ADMIN_APP_BASE_URL (plus localhost origins in development only); the new variable is added to render.yaml (both services), .env.example and the env-completeness check; the QA harness's CORS proxy and the D-01 test.fail() are removed - refs (not counted here): none | 0 | No frame counted here | QA D-01: sign-up fails in every browser when web and API are on different origins (as in production, separate Render services). registerUser sends credentials: 'include' and app.enableCors() answers '*', which browsers refuse; the console says the Access-Control-Allow-Origin header must not be the wildcard when credentials mode is 'include', and no request reaches the API. Only registerUser uses credentials, so login and the other calls work, which is why no mocked suite saw it. It blocks every onboarding journey, including the guardian-consent flow, so it is a blocker and a safeguarding blocker. | Decided 2026-10-18 (#407, item 1; founder-adopted default, veto-able); not built. The API sets no cookie and returns the refresh token in the response body, so the credentials option appears to have no function (the code comment says httpOnly cookie, Build Plan Section 5.7; confirm in the build PR before removing it). What the API does when WEB_APP_BASE_URL or ADMIN_APP_BASE_URL is unset in production (refuse to boot like assertProductionEnv, or grant no browser origin) is for the build PR to choose and state. The staging service carries its own two values. When fixed, QA test D-01 (e2e/tests/00-known-defects.spec.ts) goes red: delete its test.fail() and the harness proxy (e2e/README.md); BUILT 2026-10-18 (backend/w3e, Decision Log #407 item 1; docs/backend-w3e-report.md): services/api/src/cors-options.ts + main.ts - CORS allows only the origins of WEB_APP_BASE_URL and the new ADMIN_APP_BASE_URL (plus localhost outside production), never *, no credentials; an unset or unusable variable contributes no origin and is logged (fail closed, boot NOT refused - still undecided); ADMIN_APP_BASE_URL added to render.yaml (both services), .env.example and docs/deployment.md; apps/web registerUser no longer sends credentials: include; e2e/ CORS proxy deleted and the D-01 test.fail() removed; 2026-10-19 (#408, item 1): the open point of this row is decided: in production an unset or invalid WEB_APP_BASE_URL or ADMIN_APP_BASE_URL refuses to boot (assertProductionEnv) instead of only logging; not built, see B-163. | B-164 | Cross-tab lock for the refresh-token rotation - apps/web/src/lib/silentRefresh.ts: one tab at a time calls POST /auth/refresh, the other tabs wait and then use the tokens the first tab stored; a real two-tab test - refs (not counted here): none | 0 | No frame counted here | backend/w3e found it while stabilising a test (docs/backend-w3e-report.md sections 2 and 4): silentRefresh.ts is single-flight inside one tab only. The refresh token rotates and the server treats a second use of an old token as reuse, so two open tabs that refresh together can log the person out. W3e worked around it in one test (06-login-routing, 'an expired access token is silently refreshed': navigate to about:blank first, seed the token with an init script); it is a product bug, not a test problem. | Decided 2026-10-19 (#408, item 2; founder-adopted default, veto-able); not built. The mechanism (the Web Locks API, a storage-based lock or a BroadcastChannel) is the build PR's choice and it states its fallback where the API is missing. The tab that takes the lock re-reads the stored tokens first: if another tab already rotated them it uses the new pair and does not call the server. The alternative W3e named, a short server-side reuse grace, is not chosen; the server's reuse detection is unchanged. Add a two-tab test to e2e/ and drop the stale-page workaround from 06-login-routing if it is no longer needed; FC4 2026-10-20 (PR #435): BUILT (apps/web). silentRefresh.ts rotates under a cross-tab lock (Web Locks API; localStorage lease fallback) and re-reads the stored tokens first. Unit tests + a two-page Playwright test (fails on the old code: two refresh calls; passes now). Required scope: a glossary of every term; what the founder does versus what an agent does versus what needs counsel; the order of operations; for each part the exact click-by-click steps, which value goes in which setting, how to check it worked and what to do if it did not: Neon production and staging databases and branches, running the migrations (including the free-plan pre-deploy limitation) and the DOB backfill; Upstash Redis; generating and storing every secret (JWT_SECRET, ADMIN_JWT_SECRET, DOB_ENCRYPTION_KEY - one key protects both the date of birth and the 2FA secrets, #405 item 8 - per environment, with the loss-and-rollback implications from docs/dob-encryption-rollback.md); the API service on Render and its health check; the web and admin static sites (build command, publish directory, VITE_API_BASE_URL, rewrite rule, the build:release gate); custom domains, DNS and https for all three; CORS origins; Postmark (account, sender domain, SPF/DKIM/DMARC, the email templates and the counsel-pending wording); MaxMind GeoLite2 (account id, licence key, what happens without it); Stripe (live keys, webhook, the card-verification flow); Cloudflare R2 media storage (bucket, keys, the public base URL gap); the Highlightly sports data key and daily budget; Sentry; creating the first superadmin and admin accounts; branch and release strategy (staging versus main); the pending-copy release check and counsel sign-off; legal pages, DPIA and safeguarding sign-off items still open at that time; backups, monitoring and alerting; a post-deploy smoke test (the QA suite against staging, then production); rollback for each part; ongoing monthly costs; and a final go/no-go checklist. | B-181 | Username filter: the allow-list for nazif, nazifa and nazifah becomes an exact match on the handle as typed, plus the name followed only by digits - services/api username-rules.constants.ts and username-rules.util.ts - refs (not counted here): none | 0 | No frame counted here | B-165 built the allow-list as squash(raw) equality, so underscore forms (naz_ifa, _nazif_, nazi_f) also pass, and the digit forms (nazif_10, nazifa2) are refused (docs/backend-w3f-report.md section 3). | Decided 2026-10-20 (#410, item 2; founder-adopted default, veto-able); not built. Rule: (a) the handle as typed, compared case-insensitively, equals nazif, nazifa or nazifah; NO underscore variants (naz_ifa, _nazif_, nazif_a stay refused); (b) additionally the name followed ONLY by digits is accepted, the decision's examples being nazifa2 and nazif_10. Adopted reading of (b), because one example has an underscore: the handle, as typed, matches ^(nazif|nazifa|nazifah)_?[0-9]+$ (any number of digits, at most one underscore, between the name and the digits). Everything else stays refused: nazifan, nazifan_10, nazif_an, nazifah_fan, xxnazifxx, xnazifa, nazifax, nazif__10, n4zif (no digit-for-letter forms). NOT decided: a limit on the number of digits; digits before the name. Build notes: check (b) BEFORE the CONTAINS rule runs on the squashed form (squash removes underscores, so nazif_10 becomes nazif10 and would trip 'nazi'); keep every other term refused (nazifhitler and nazif_porn stay refused by the other terms); change the W3f tests: naz_ifa and _nazif_ move from accepted to refused, nazifa2 and nazif_10 move from refused to accepted. The term lists, the 5-letter threshold and the innocent-word list are unchanged and stay open (B-165); 2026-10-24 (#411, item 9; correction of record): B-181 had no build prompt after A8 (PR #437); it is built in the next backend PR. Still not built; 2026-10-24 (backend/w6-username-cover-block, docs/backend-w6-report.md; #411 item 9): BUILT. isAllowedNameHandle: the handle AS TYPED, case-insensitive, is nazif / nazifa / nazifah, or one of them followed only by digits with at most one underscore between (^(nazif|nazifa|nazifah)_?[0-9]+$); NO underscore removal; exempts only the guarded nazi CONTAINS rule. Tests moved as decided: naz_ifa and _nazif_ now refused (also nazi_f, nazif_a), nazifa2 and nazif_10 now accepted; nazifan, nazifan_10, nazif_an, xnazifa, nazif__10, 10nazif stay refused, and nazifhitler / nazif_porn stay refused by the other rules. NOT decided and left refused: a limit on the number of digits; digits before the name; 2026-10-25 (#412, item 6): the digit rules W6 built stand - any number of digits after the name with at most one underscore between, digits before the name refused, no cap on the number of digits | #410 (item 2), #408 (item 3), #407 (item 3), #369 | B-165 | none | BE -> QA | W4 | N |

## 3. Waves

Every B-ID is in exactly one wave (the wave in which its work is finished). Where a row needs a Figma step earlier than its code step, the Figma step is its own W0 row and the code row depends on it. A row never depends on a later wave (checked by the generator).

### W0 Figma pass

Reason: Design must stop contradicting the decisions before anything is built against it: Age Gate + country, Register read-only DOB, Create Profile, other-user profile, guardian-flow copy, the declined-minor frame, email links, 2FA, new admin actions. Net-new screens for later waves (kickoff-alert control, banter status/topics) are designed at the start of their own wave.

B-IDs (57): B-001 (f_agegate), B-002 (f_gcflow), B-003 (f_decline), B-004 (f_cge), B-005 (f_register), B-006 (f_createprofile), B-007 (f_profile), B-008 (f_login), B-009 (f_email), B-010 (f_legal), B-011 (f_sports), B-012 (f_adminnew), B-013 (f_withdraw), B-014 (f_acct), B-015 (f_2fa), B-095 (f_admin_contest), B-096 (f_contest_states), B-104 (f_dob_admin), B-105 (f_pwd_email), B-107 (f_resync_2026_10_16), B-113 (f_resync_2026_10_16b), B-119 (f_admin_incorrect_details), B-121 (f_notif_contest), B-135 (f_approval_on_hold), B-136 (f_verify_prompt), B-137 (f_dob_guardian_fields), B-138 (f_profile_general), B-144 (k2_archive_saved), B-145 (k2_media_saved_states), B-146 (k2_on_hold_gaps), B-147 (k2_dob_confirm_success, OPEN), B-148 (k2_profile_open, OPEN), B-149 (k2_grey_token, OPEN), B-150 (k2_verify_prompt_copy, OPEN), B-167 (f_revoke_hold_line), B-170 (f_profile_k4), B-177 (f_guardian_follow_footnote), B-183 (logged_out_components), B-184 (mobile_sample_values), B-188 (k2_notes), B-190 (withdraw_wording_check), B-191 (k5_restricted_pending), B-192 (held_until_date), B-195 (preferred_club_frame), B-198 (restricted_pending_k6), B-201 (own_post_handles), B-202 (logged_out_links), B-203 (k5_avatar_check), B-204 (drawer_standalone), B-209 (fp_prefclub), B-210 (fp_cover_crop), B-211 (fp_image_rejected), B-212 (fp_tag_review), B-218 (block_label_figma), B-222 (f_cover_crop_states), B-223 (f_withdraw_prefill), B-224 (f_image_notif_both)

### W1 Backend foundations

Reason: Nothing user-visible works end to end until the emails carry links, a session survives registration, and the environment is complete. These are prerequisites of every gate and flow below.

B-IDs (13): B-016 (email_links), B-017 (session), B-018 (env_render), B-019 (admin_env), B-020 (verify_enforce), B-021 (consent_lookup), B-022 (sports_e2e), B-115 (pwd_email_link), B-116 (jest_workers), B-140 (qa_cors), B-141 (qa_age5_floor), B-163 (prod_origin_boot), B-225 (withdraw_link_throttle)

### W2 Country / geo

Reason: Country must exist (declared at the Age Gate, derived at Register) before the safeguarding policy and OAuth can rely on it.

B-IDs (3): B-023 (country_schema), B-024 (geoip), B-025 (country_policy)

### W3 Gates

Reason: Gates sit on top of W1-W2: uniform write block, profile-complete gate, minor approval email, wrong-DOB flag, routing into the activation flow.

B-IDs (16): B-026 (write_block), B-027 (profile_gate), B-028 (approval_email), B-029 (wrongdob), B-030 (activation_flow), B-102 (decline_cap), B-103 (dob_correction), B-109 (admin_user_detail), B-110 (declined_login; re-scoped by #402 to sessions_between_declines), B-118 (verify_social_writes), B-126 (declined_no_expiry, OPEN), B-158 (w3d_email_wording), B-160 (w3d_old_links, OPEN), B-161 (w3d_nonactive_stale, OPEN), B-171 (w3d_verify_fields, OPEN), B-172 (admin_user_age_tier, OPEN)

### W4 Username

Reason: Username is required for the incomplete-profile gate to mean anything; moderation and cursor leaks must be closed before usernames are exposed more widely.

B-IDs (7): B-031 (username_req), B-032 (username_mod), B-033 (username_revoke), B-034 (cursor_leaks), B-142 (qa_username_contains), B-165 (username_nazif), B-181 (nazif_allowlist_v2)

### W5 Profile and media

Reason: Profile and media need R2 (and Rekognition) and the new columns before Create Profile, profile pages and post attachments can be built.

## 5. Counsel-pending items that gate rows

From `docs/legal-copy-draft-tos-privacy-policy.md` Part C (v0.8). The draft is NOT APPROVED; the only counsel position recorded for v0.8 is "founder states counsel agrees" to guardian contact for all minors. A "gates" entry means the row should not ship its user-facing wording or data handling until counsel has reviewed it; it does not block building behind a flag.

## 6. Unresolved

Items I could not determine or that the inputs contradict. None is guessed.

### 6a. Audit inconsistencies

- **Audit date.** `docs/mvp-coverage-audit.md` header says 2026-10-08; CLAUDE.md's `audit/mvp-coverage-audit` bullet says 2026-10-10. The audit's base commit is `06dcaa0` (PR #388 merge); `origin/main` has since moved (`a054e71`). I could not tell which date is correct.
- **"No frame" vs Q4 frame.** Audit "Top gaps" item 4 says viewing another user's profile has "no frame and no route", but Q4 and the frame table show Figma frames `1455:4362`, `1455:6626`, `1460:8940`, `5702:8250`, `5778:8490`, `5778:8567` (a viewer-facing profile with Follow, message, Copy link, Block). The frames exist; the route does not. This register follows Q4 and the table. Whether those same frames double as the owner's own profile is unanswered in the audit.
- **Overstated Q7 DM contradiction.** Q7/Q14.12 contrasts "Under-18 accounts cannot receive ordinary direct messages" (guardian screen) with "a minor can send DMs". That mixes sending and receiving. Code (per Q9/Q14.10): under-16s are blocked from messaging entirely; an adult cannot start a new thread with a minor (404); restricted-pending minors cannot send or be messaged; a 16-17-year-old can still be messaged by other minors. So the copy is imprecise (and Q14.10's "only accounts they follow can start a DM" has no code behind it), but the stark contradiction as phrased is overstated.
- **342 vs 443.** The audit's 342 is screen frames (Built + gaps); Reference (65) and Archived (36) are on top of it, giving the audit's 443 total. The brief's reconciliation formula (rows + Built + Reference + Archived = 342) cannot hold literally; Section 1 reconciles both figures.
- **Tag counts.** The audit's "other 78" counts Partial + Stub + Missing only; the 6 NotInspected frames are all tagged `other` and are not in that total (84 with them). Recount confirms every other tag count.
- **Archived bucket.** `1870:2753` ("Old - Mobile Drop Down Components") is counted Archived although it is not hidden (the audit notes this); I left it as the audit has it.
- **Frame-only counts.** All 196 Built frames were only checked for route/page/endpoint existence, not copy or pixels (audit Method); any of them may still be stale against #367-#380 beyond the ones this register names.

### 6b. Decision-log gaps that block or shape rows

- **#378 vs Competition deferral.** #378 makes every Stub launch-blocking, including the Competition tab and Admin Create Competition (6 frames, `competition` row). Build Plan 2.2 and Decision Log #72/#73 defer the Competition umbrella and no data model, scoring or admin endpoint exists. Which wins is not stated. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#392 item 1): build it; Competition umbrella and Create Competition are in scope (B-100).**
- **Contest voting.** 6 Missing frames (`contest_core`) depict community voting; no Decision Log entry I found defines a voting mechanic (the contest is admin-judged). I did not locate a spec; the docx search for "vote" returned only #72, #130, #134, #238, #272. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#381, #387, #392 item 2): voting is built, contests are not admin-judged; spec in docs/contest-spec.md (B-091, B-098).**
- **Community Group feed.** 4 Partial frames show a group feed; Decision Log #281/#294 (existing) say none is built or intended. #378 says Partial is in scope. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#392 item 3): build the group feed; supersedes #281/#294 (B-101).**
- **#369 open points.** Placeholder name for incomplete profiles, and treatment of legacy accounts with no username, are explicitly open. **Partly resolved 2026-10-14 (#392 item 9): legacy accounts without a username must complete the profile before accessing the platform; the placeholder name stays open.**
- **#367 open points.** No self-service correction for a mistyped DOB once read-only; where Preferred Club free text is stored (no column; `clubAffiliationId` is an FK to a club page); what "Create Profile is blocked for restricted-pending" looks like on screen. **DOB-correction point resolved 2026-10-14 (#390; B-103, B-104). Preferred Club storage resolved 2026-10-15 (#392 item 8 correction: new free-text field, B-038). The on-screen block for restricted-pending remains open.**
- **#368 open points.** Which table holds declared/derived/method/timestamp (User or Guardian or both); who owns GeoLite2 licence terms and refresh; whether the derived country is also used for non-US demographic grouping beyond what is described.
- **#372, #375, #376.** Counsel proportionality of returning a child's DOB to a token holder; what an open wrong-DOB flag does to the consent flow; the exact "no content" test for purge; the 6-month and appeal-window figures are not counsel-sourced and conflict with the legal copy's 6-7 year moderation retention (PP s9).
- **#371.** CSAM hash-matching coverage on R2 and statutory duties for hosting images of minors; Rekognition EU/UK region availability; whether Rekognition also applies to post attachments (`post_attach`, `bants_create`).
- **#374.** "Archive or rewrite" frame `5491:8241`/`5501:8453` - no choice recorded. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#389): rewrite and wire the frames to the decline-cap logic (B-102, B-003).**
- **#379 vs code.** #379 says items 1, 5, 11, 13, 14 are "addressed by the flow, copy and legal-draft changes in this same batch", but that batch was docs only; the code still contains "Add your position and photo" (`GuardianConsentPage.tsx:299`) and the "player profile" claim, so item 11 is not fixed in code (rows `gc_pending_fe`, `gc_consent_fe`).
- **#378 and NotInspected.** #378 names Missing, Stub and Partial; the 6 NotInspected frames are not mentioned. They are placed in W8 as verification. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#392 item 5): verify them (B-106).**
- **Password-change emails.** `1380:2297` ("password change requested") describes an email-link flow that does not exist and overlaps `1380:2318`; which of the two, or neither, should ship is not decided. **Resolved 2026-10-15 (#392 item 4 correction, #393): both frames ship. 1380:2318 = confirmation email after a password reset is completed; 1380:2297 = security alert when a suspicious password change occurs. Copy work in the Figma pass (B-105).**

### 6c. Frames whose spec contradicts a decision where the intended side is unclear

- `5491:8241` / `5501:8453` decline frames - decision #374 contradicts them; archive vs rewrite open. **Resolved 2026-10-14 (#389): rewrite and wire; max 3 declines.**
- Guardian Details `5108:6627` / `5539:7354` - Figma (single name, no country) agrees with #63 and #368, code disagrees; I treated the Figma as intended (row `guardian_details_fe`). **Resolved by founder 2026-10-15 (#393 (a)): Option A, single full-name field and no country select; the code changes (B-075).**
- Register `407:1051` / `1625:2333` and Create Profile - frames contradict #367 (DOB picker, Location); treated as stale, but the audit notes the frames have Username/Full-name rows that differ by breakpoint and I could not tell which breakpoint is intended. **Resolved by founder 2026-10-15 (#392 item 10 correction): 390px is the canonical breakpoint; stale frames are normalised to it in the Figma pass (B-005, B-006).**
- Change Guardian Email `5498:7164` / `5501:8536` - code (built under #365) has name + relationship, the frames do not; I treated the code as intended (row `f_cge`) because #365 is a recorded resolution, but the frames were never updated. **Resolved by founder 2026-10-15 (#393 (b)): the code is intended and the frames are updated to add guardian name and relationship (B-004).**
- Create Profile mobile `1629:2449` is 430px wide, not the canonical 390 (audit Q14.14). **Resolved 2026-10-15 (#392 item 10 correction): normalise to 390 (B-006).**
- `5108:6630` carries a "SCOPE OPEN" note about Grassroots/Sports Hub limits for restricted-pending users; nothing records it as resolved.
- `5108:6631` lists "DMs on" among things switched on; whether that is correct for 16-17 vs under-16 minors depends on the DM rules above and I could not confirm the intended message.
- Bants create-topic frames `2355:2137`, `5651:8166` are Partial but the audit's note only explains the attachment variants.
- Bants post page `2256:11081` / `5651:8253` - audit says built "with no matching Figma frame" yet maps frames as closest; the intended difference is unclear.
- Dashboard `110:5` "New users by league" - `User` has no league attribute; no spec for the grouping.
- Delete Role `5403:7205` - no delete endpoint; semantics of deleting a staff account undefined.
- Settings Display leaf merge (Display into Accessibility) is an open founder call (#246, existing).

### 6d. Not mapped / not verifiable from the repo

- The 122 non-frame top-level Figma nodes (symbols, banners, loose text) were not mapped (audit s6). The "Future Features" banner (`6527:21127`, `6527:21125`) was not inspected, so it is unknown which frames sit under it (audit Q14.16).
- Hidden `Community Home Page Template` `1306:7149` and instance `1308:11643` (`visible: false`) are in the audit but were not assigned any work.
- Whether any external account in Section 4 exists, and whether any key is set in the real Render environments, is not visible from the repo.
- Whether the API boots when `ADMIN_JWT_SECRET` is unset was not run by the audit and not run here.
- The sports e2e key-isolation issue (`sports_e2e`) is taken from CLAUDE.md's bullets; I did not re-run the e2e suite.
- Test suites were not run for this document (docs only).
- Effort and sequencing inside a wave were not estimated; waves give order and reasons only.

### 10b. New register rows and why they are new

| New row | Why it is not an extension of an existing row |
|---|---|
| B-107 | The Figma legs of B-001, B-002, B-003, B-006, B-007, B-009, B-012, B-013, B-015, B-095, B-096 and B-104 are already marked done by W0 passes A-E; re-opening each would have mislabelled finished work. One consolidated pass, with a status line on each touched row. |
| B-108 | B-039 builds the Block model and endpoints (extended); B-082 builds the other-user profile page and lists Block as one action; neither covers the Settings blocked-users list, unblock or the reason field UI. |
| B-109 | B-012 and B-104 designed the User Detail host screen but no row builds the read endpoint or the screen; B-103 and B-033 both need them and sit in W3 and W4, so this row is W3. |
| B-110 | B-102 covers the counter and resend after a decline; no row covers a login that answers 'declined' instead of a token. [2026-10-16, #402: that login result is withdrawn; B-110 is re-scoped to keeping sessions valid between declines 1 and 2 and is a prerequisite of B-102; see Section 11.] |
| B-111 | B-086 (TOTP backend) and B-087 (screens) cover enrol and code entry; recovery and the login-time prompt are separate surfaces and are Figma-undrawn. |
| B-112 | B-013 (Figma) and B-080 (page) cover the withdrawal page; the link in the consent email and on the approved screen, and the throttle and new-link behaviours, are not in either. |

Not new rows, on purpose (existing row already covers the work): username revoke backend (B-033, extended; B-032 holds the 30-day rule); Block backend (B-039, extended); the contest scheduler for the 20:00 lock and 08:00 opening and the weekly result calculation (B-091, which already names the phase and reveal schedulers); admin Contest form changes (B-095, B-097); vote toggle (B-098).

Owners and waves follow the register's own rules: Figma steps are W0 and DS -> SB; a row never depends on a later wave, so B-109 and B-110 (prerequisites of B-103, W3) are W3, B-108 and B-112 (consumers of W5/W9 rows) are W9, B-111 (consumer of B-086/B-087) is W10.

### 10d. Still open (rewritten 2026-10-16 by Decision Log #402; updated again by #403 and #404 - lists only what remains open)

Decided and therefore removed from this list: the 'declined' login result and its BACKEND TO CONFIRM (withdrawn, B-110 re-scoped); whether the third decline skips the 30-day grace (it does not); whether 'cannot re-pick' a revoked username is permanent (yes, for that user; 30 days for everyone else); whether a blocked user is told (no), the effects of Block on comments, likes, search, rosters and the leaderboard, and whether unblock restores follows (it does not); the backup-code count and the lost-device recovery proof; whether tolerance and the per-viewer cap are per contest or week 4 only (week 4 only). These are founder-adopted defaults and veto-able (Section 11a); only the founder's veto reopens them.

Also decided and removed by #403 (Section 12): the placeholder name for incomplete profiles ('Soccernity member'); whether a DOB-correction restart resets the decline counter (it does, with an audit entry); the withdrawal throttle window (5 attempts per 15 minutes, configuration); whether the Already Voted page remains (it is archived; the Voting page adopts its design); whether the Community-feed 'Final week' module remains (it does, as a carousel); whether Block affects Bants rooms and like mechanics (hides posts, comments and like identities in both directions, counts unchanged, no room membership).

Also decided and removed by #404 (Section 14): the forfeit pop-up reading of 'Contest page' (Contest details and Voting) and several forfeited weeks (one after another, oldest first); the vote-toggle count and banner (live by one, no banner); whether carousel views count as impressions (yes, when a tile is actually visible); how other users reach a finalist's entry after the reveal (finalist names open the profile, carousel tiles open the entry); what the Contest tab shows from 00:00 to 08:00 on day 1 (Between Weeks, 'Week 1 opens at 08:00'); the 2FA backup-code format and the entry points of the manual key and Turn off; what a blocked user sees of the blocker's profile (not found); how Block interacts with #346 and #347 (the age rules apply first; a block never reveals itself); the Bants mapping questions (thread = BanterRoom, no membership to post, join = bookmark, moderation = reports plus admin); which routes count as social writes (the built table in docs/backend-w3-gates-report.md); the W3 judgment calls 1-7 (adopted); the Figma direction for the desktop profile frames (the new auto-layout ones) and for the admin components (move, then adopt).

Counsel and legal:

- Counsel wording for every [COPY PENDING LEGAL REVIEW] item (A2, A4, A5, B3, C3, D3 and the new Figma text in B-107), including the third-decline email and the username-revoke email.
- The CSAM / statutory path for flagged images (#371, D2).
- 2FA secret and backup-code storage, counsel (legal copy Part C row 44). [2026-10-17, #405: storage decided (item 8); counsel remains.]
- Contest notification wording that reaches minors (B-121, B-122, B-123); no Part C row yet.

Decisions not yet made:

- The number of days / window lengths stated as "configuration" other than the withdrawal throttle (decided: 5 attempts per 15 minutes, #403).
- 2FA: storage and encryption of the TOTP secret itself; the backup-code character set (the founder said '5-5 groups'); whether the 5-tries lock is per account (assumed) or per IP, since a per-account lock lets a stranger lock out an account holder. [2026-10-17, #405: decided - item 8: the TOTP secret is encrypted with the existing date-of-birth encryption; the backup-code character set is upper-case letters and digits without 0, O, 1, I; the lock is per account. Counsel remains.]
- Carousel (#404 items 6, 15): what 'actually visible' means in numbers for an impression (share of the tile in view, time in view); whether the entry appears in the Community feed once the pin is removed at the end of the month (item 9 says it becomes an ordinary post, item 15 says only that the pin is removed). [2026-10-17, #405: decided - items 1 and 7: an impression is at least 50% of the tile in view for at least 1 second; once the pin is removed the entry reaches the Community feed as an ordinary post. Not stated: whether the second is continuous and whether re-entry counts again.]
- Bants (#404 item 10): whether a like-identities list exists (assumed none, to confirm); the build change that removes the membership check on posting is owed to the build PR, not a decision.
- Admin shell search bar: build vs hide (B-114).
- Contest notifications (#404 item 16): the timing of (d) and (f) is assumed (week-3 result; end of the contest month); whether an unverified-email account receives them; the idempotency key (BACKEND TO CONFIRM, B-122). [2026-10-17, #405: closed - item 4: accounts with an unverified email receive none; the idempotency key is Notification.dedupeKey (B-122, built, PR #417); the timing of (d) and (f) is still ASSUMED.]
- Figma follow-ups of #404: whether the blocked user's 'not found' profile needs a new frame or reuses the app's not-found state; legacy profile frames archived (default) rather than deleted; the admin components move and their adoption on the Pass C / Pass F1 frames (B-113, B-095). [2026-10-17, #405: decided - item 9: the legacy profile frames are archived, not deleted; item 10: Field, the eight Dialogs and the eight Tables stay local frames and the 257 hidden ARCHIVED originals stay until the launch gate (B-090). The 'not found' frame question is still open.]
- B-119: whether resolving an incorrect-details report needs a note or a confirmation step.
- Whether the restored 'Closed (not a finalist)' copy may reveal anything about uploads before the reveal (B-113).
- Crest licensing in the Fixtures module of the Create Post frames (#85, #97); the typographic marks are accepted for now (#403 item 13).
- Counsel: country display (reduced scope, #403 item 17); the new Approved-screen wording and the decline-page cap sentence ([COPY PENDING LEGAL REVIEW]).
- B-109: the audit field list for the DOB tool and username revoke ("specified in the build PR"); which admin roles may read a decrypted DOB. [2026-10-17, #405: closed - item 5: moderator and superadmin; the audit fields as built are recorded in B-103 and B-109 (PR #416).]
- Carried over: B-100 scoring spec, B-101 group-feed model, Log Book sections 6.3, 23.3 and 24.3.
- [2026-10-17, #405] Open: the exact scope of the pending-copy guard (B-124, named by the founder without its definition); whether a row declined at 1 or 2 should expire, be reminded or be left alone (B-126); the time of day of the pin-removal flip; whether the 1-second view must be continuous; whether the 257 archived originals are deleted at the launch gate.

## 11. Updates 2026-10-16 (founder-adopted defaults, Decision Log #402)

Source: Decision Log #402 (`docs/Soccernity_MVP_Build_Plan_v1.7.docx`, Section 9) and `docs/contest-spec.md` Sections 4, 4a, 10b, 14c and 16. Everything below is DECIDED, NOT BUILT. Items 2-6 are founder-adopted defaults, labelled veto-able. No B-ID was added or removed; B-110 was re-scoped in place.

### 11a. Coverage

| # | Item | Adopted as | Settles | Register rows |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Decline 1 and 2 | Sessions stay valid between declines (#389); the minor sees the existing decline page INSIDE the account via the existing authenticated resend / change-guardian-email routes; the 'declined' login result is withdrawn; GuardianConsentPage stops treating 'declined' as pending | Amends #395 B1; withdraws its BACKEND TO CONFIRM (B1) | B-110 (re-scoped), B-102, B-003, B-107 |
| 2 | Third decline | Login blocked at once; pending deletion with the existing 30-day grace (#375) | #395 open question on skipping the grace | B-102, B-003 |
| 3 | Revoked username | Held 30 days for everyone; the revoked user can never re-pick it | #398 D3 ambiguity | B-032, B-033 |
| 4 | Block | Blocked user not told; hides content and search results in both directions (posts, comments, likes, search, rosters); leaderboard unaffected; unblock does not restore follows | #396 C2 open points (all but Bants rooms, like mechanics, #346 / #347 interaction) | B-039, B-108, B-107 |
| 5 | 2FA | 10 single-use backup codes, stored hashed; lost-device recovery needs a backup code, or email confirmation plus password | #397 C3 open points (count, recovery proof, backup-code storage) | B-086, B-087, B-111 |
| 6 | Week-4 exposure settings | Tolerance and per-viewer daily cap apply to week 4 only (#388); ranges kept as validation bounds; they lock when week 4 starts | #399 E4/E5 open point | B-091, B-092, B-095, B-097 |

Dated notes were appended (original text unchanged) to Decision Log #395, #396, #397, #398 and #399. #389 and #375 are referenced, not edited. #400 and #401 are unaffected.

### 11b. B-110 re-scope

- Before: declined-login result (POST /auth/login answers 'declined' with no token; BACKEND TO CONFIRM how resend / change-guardian-email are authorised without a session).
- After: keep sessions valid between declines 1 and 2, revoke only on the third, fix 'declined' rendering. Same B-ID, same wave (W3), same owners (BE -> FC -> QA).
- Dependencies: B-110 was "B-102, B-003, B-107"; now "B-003, B-107". B-102 was "B-003, B-030"; now "B-003, B-030, B-110". The chain is B-107 -> B-003 -> B-110 -> B-102; there is no cycle and no row depends on a later wave.
- Build implication, recorded not decided: resend and change-guardian-email refuse a declined account today (#365), and every decline revokes sessions today (#337-#340); both must change in the build PR.

## 36. Updates 2026-10-25 (backend: Notification.actorId, B-213)

`backend/notification-actor-id`. `services/api` only: one additive migration (`20261025120000_add_notification_actor_id`, rollback in its header, no backfill: the platform is not live), no new environment variable, no frontend, no Figma, no `.docx`. Status lines were appended (original text unchanged) to the Spec-status cell of B-213 and B-197. No B-ID added or removed (217 B-IDs; 146 gap frames unchanged).

### 36a. What B-213 now is

- BUILT. `Notification.actorId` (nullable plain string, no foreign key, index on `(userId, actorId)`) is written by `FeedService.likePost` and `FeedService.addComment` inside the existing transactions: the liker or commenter, never the recipient. No other notification type sets it.
- Read side: `notificationBlockHiddenFilter` (list, unread count, read-all, mark-one-read) now also leaves out a `like` or `comment` row whose `actorId` is the other party of a Block, in either direction. Hidden at read time, never deleted; the rows reappear on unblock.
- System notifications (`moderation_decision`, `contest_*`, `age_milestone`, `match_kickoff`) have no user actor and are not filtered.
- Rows with a NULL `actorId` (written before the column) stay visible. This needed an explicit `actorId: { not: null, ... }` in the filter: the exclusion is wrapped in `NOT(...)`, and in SQL `NOT (type IN (...) AND actorId IN (...))` is NULL, not true, for a NULL actor, which silently hid every such row. Found by the e2e test with a NULL-actor row, not by the unit tests (which assert the generated filter, not its SQL meaning).
- Tests: unit (the two write sites assert `actorId`; the filter clause, the not-null guard, and that no system type is named), and an e2e against real Postgres (`test/profile-block.e2e-spec.ts`: actor recorded and never the recipient, hidden from both the blocker and the blocked recipient, unread count and read-all consistent, hidden rows stay unread in the database, reappear on unblock, NULL-actor and `moderation_decision` rows unfiltered).

### 36b. Not changed, still as before

- The "Someone" wording of like and comment rows: naming the actor is not decided, and `actorId` is never selected into an API payload.
- No B-ID added; B-197's other open points (blocked reporter / reported pair and admin decisions, Bants listing) are unchanged.

## 39. Updates 2026-10-25 (backend/w8-image-rejection-reason: B-214, the rejection reason and the image-decision notification)

`services/api` only: one additive migration (`20261025150000_add_profile_image_rejection_reason`, rollback in its header), no new environment variable, no frontend, no Figma, no `.docx`. Status lines were appended (original text unchanged) to the Spec-status cell of B-214, B-217 and B-211. No B-ID added or removed (221 B-IDs; 146 gap frames unchanged).

### 39a. What B-214 now is

- BUILT, with no caller yet. `UserImageReviewService.rejectProfileImage(userId, kind, reason)` (`kind` is `'avatar'` or `'cover'`) is the method the review queue (B-037) and the image check (B-036) will call. Nothing in the running app calls it today: the admin reject endpoint and screen are not built here. UsersModule exports the service.
- One transaction does three things: the image goes to status `'rejected'` (from `'pending'` or `'approved'`), the reason is stored on the same user row, and one Notification is written. The status test is in the WHERE of the write, so two simultaneous rejections produce one winner and one notification; the loser gets a 409. A user with no such image gets a 404.
- Storage: nullable `User.avatarRejectionReason` and `User.coverRejectionReason`. CHECK constraints: a reason may exist only while that image's status is `'rejected'`, and is never blank. `'rejected'` was already inside the existing status CHECK, so those constraints are unchanged.
- The reason: admin-written text, required, trimmed, at most 300 characters (an application rule, adopted default, veto-able; the database does not freeze the number).
- Owner-only exposure: the reason is returned as `avatar.rejectionReason` and `cover.rejectionReason` on the owner's own `GET /users/:id` (null unless the image is rejected). The avatar and cover upload responses carry `rejectionReason: null` (an upload clears it). No other-user read selects it: the public profile does not, and `GET /users/:id` for anyone else is a 403. The storage key is still never returned.
- Cleared by the next upload, by a delete and by anonymization (the CHECK needs it to move with the status).
- The notification: new `Notification.type` value `profile_image_rejected`, `payloadRefId` is `'avatar'` or `'cover'`. It does NOT copy the reason, so a stale copy cannot outlive the next upload. `GET /notifications` resolves it to `data: { image: 'avatar' | 'cover' }`, with no extra query; an unrecognised `payloadRefId` resolves to `data: null`. In-app only: no email, no push (#412 item 2).
- Tests: unit (the service: both kinds, trimming, the length boundary, every invalid reason refused before any database work, 409, 404, rollback on a notification failure; the owner read and the public read in `users.service.w5.spec.ts`; the avatar, cover and anonymization write shapes; the notification resolver). e2e against real Postgres with a fake object store (`test/profile-image-rejection.e2e-spec.ts`, 23 tests: both kinds through the real upload endpoints, the reason on the owner's `GET /users/:id` and nowhere else, the notification through the real `GET /notifications`, re-upload and delete clearing the reason, an approved image rejected later, a double and a simultaneous rejection, the CHECK constraints refusing a stale or blank reason, anonymization).

### 39b. Not built, and decisions this pass did not make

- The admin endpoint and screen that call the method (B-037, B-012), the automatic image check (B-036), and everything in `apps/web` (B-215, B-217). No route was invented.
- Adopted, not stated by the founder: the type name `profile_image_rejected` (a future approved-image notification would be a separate type or a later decision); a rejection of an already-rejected image is a 409, not an overwrite; an `'approved'` image can still be rejected.
- The old notification stays in the Notification Centre after a re-upload (it is history; the reason it points at is gone). Whether it should be removed on re-upload is not decided.
- Open under B-214, unchanged: whether a reason written by a moderator and shown to a possible child needs counsel wording guidance (adopted: admin-written, not tagged).

### 40b. What the code checks found (2026-10-25)

- **The withdrawal-request email already links with the token pre-filled.** `RegistrationEmailService.sendGuardianWithdrawalRequestEmail` builds `<WEB_APP_BASE_URL>/guardian-consent/withdraw?token=<withdrawalToken>` and also keeps the code in the body (B-016 built it). B-112's "BACKEND TO CONFIRM what the consent-email link can pre-fill" is answered: the consent email carries no withdrawal link, so nothing is pre-filled there; the pre-fill is the withdrawal email's, and the missing piece is a page that honours `?token=` (FC6).
- **The two withdrawal routes use a bare `@AuthRateLimit()`.** They share the module-wide `auth` throttle (`AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` / `AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_MS`, default 5 per 60 s), not 5 per 15 minutes and not a setting of their own. B-225 gives them their own configurable throttle (the variable names are a proposal).
- **No version bump is needed for the links.** The CONSENT_SCREEN_VERSION tripwire hashes the wording of `GuardianConsentConfirmPage.tsx` only. The consent email is a different file. A link on the Approved screen is shown after consent is recorded, so it adds nothing to the evidence the version exists to record (#348). **Risk, flagged:** if FC6 puts the link's label inside `GuardianConsentConfirmPage.tsx`, the tripwire fails and the version has to move, which the founder said not to do. The adopted default is to render the link from its own component or constant outside the hashed file (the card-verification panel is the precedent); if that is not possible FC6 stops and asks.
- **FC5 built a centre-crop, not a crop step.** The cover is cropped to 796/192 around the centre with no user control and no minimum-width refusal. Pass M drew a crop step and a mobile crop frame at 390:120 (6812:29396); a single saved crop shown cover-fit on mobile supersedes that mobile frame.
- **Pass M's image-decision row says "picture" only** ("Your picture wasn't approved - see why in Edit Profile"). The backend notification carries `data.image` = `'avatar'` or `'cover'` and no reason text (Section 39), so one row can serve both.
- **Points code that B-094 touches** (grep): `awardPoints` calls in `feed.service.ts` (`createPost`) and `users.service.ts` (`followUser`); `POST_CREATED: 3` and `FOLLOW_MADE: 1` in `points.constants.ts`; `engagement_post` and `engagement_follow` in `leaderboard.constants.ts`; tests in `points.util.spec.ts`, `feed.service.spec.ts`, `users.service.spec.ts`, `test/contest.e2e-spec.ts` and `test/leaderboard.e2e-spec.ts`.
